# Reconciliation ledger — ABILITIES (554 rows)

One row per `content/abilities/*.json` doc. Generated by the phase-2 pass of task #78; see [`README.md`](README.md) for the method, the source artefacts and the verdict rules.

Reading the *native* column: `RAWCODE ◄ BASE → engine CODE 「Blizzard comment」 Nlv`, then the merged per-level table. Values with no marker come from the **map's own `war3map.w3a` override**; values marked `*(slk)*` come from **Blizzard's `Units\AbilityData.slk` default** (extracted live from `War3Patch.mpq`) and are numbers the importer has never once read. Field labels are the real World Editor labels resolved from `AbilityMetaData.slk` + `WorldEditStrings.txt`.

Reading the *JASS* column: **A** = a named `GetSpellAbilityId()`-gated trigger (the summary is auto-extracted — treat it as an index into the named trigger, not a transcript); **B** = the ability is a `GetUnitAbilityLevel()` gate inside a global damage/attack trigger; **C** = never scripted, so the WC3 object data **is** the whole ability and a 1:1 port needs no code; **D** = needs a manual read; **E** = no w3x origin.

---

## Part 1 — the 13 seeded demo champions (65 abilities)

These are the only champions a player can currently pick (`apps/platform/internal/curation/starter.go:184-205`).

> ⚠️ **The rows below are the PHASE-2 AUDIT SNAPSHOT** — "what GGD implements TODAY" describes the
> docs *as the audit found them*, which is the point of the record. **30 of these 65 have since been
> repaired (phase 3); the current state, the source each number came from, and every remaining
> `BLOCKED` row are in [`README.md` §10](README.md#10-phase-3--deltas-closed).** Verdicts:

> | | match | partial | wrong | missing |
> |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
> | audited (phase 2) | 6 | 26 | 24 | 9 |
> | **shipping now (phase 3)** | **19** | **24** | **13** | **9** |
>
